//-- fenst/cask2d.rs -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
//! 2D visual layout engine for Xplr tree hierarchies using Cask abstractions.
//!
//! Provides multi-modal 2D layout calculation (NestedBoxes, HorizontalTree, VerticalTree, IndentedList)
//! for any tree implementing the `Xplr` trait, decoupled from any GUI framework.

use crate::fenst::cask::{
    Cask, CaskRenderCommand, Color, LayoutAndRenderCask, LayoutDirection, Padding, Point,
    Rect, Sizing,
};
use crate::fenst::provider::XplrRegistry;
use crate::fenst::xplr::Xplr;

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Visual layout strategy for arranging an `Xplr` tree in 2 dimensions.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
pub enum Cask2dLayoutMode {
    /// Recursive container windows where directories enclose their children (Clay/Cask style).
    #[default]
    NestedBoxes,
    /// 2D node-link tree hierarchy branching horizontally from left to right with connector lines.
    HorizontalTree,
    /// 2D node-link tree hierarchy branching vertically from top to bottom with connector lines.
    VerticalTree,
    /// Structured hierarchical outline list with branch indentation guides.
    IndentedList,
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Color palette for rendering 2D Cask layouts.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Cask2dColorScheme {
    pub background_root: Color,
    pub background_branch: Color,
    pub background_leaf: Color,
    pub border_color: Color,
    pub border_highlight: Color,
    pub text_primary: Color,
    pub text_secondary: Color,
    pub accent_color: Color,
    pub connector_color: Color,
}

impl Default for Cask2dColorScheme {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::DefaultDark()
    }
}

impl Cask2dColorScheme {
    /// Dark theme color scheme (Catppuccin Mocha / dark slate inspired).
    pub fn DefaultDark() -> Self {
        Self {
            background_root: Color::Hex(0x1E1E2E),
            background_branch: Color::Hex(0x252739),
            background_leaf: Color::Hex(0x313244),
            border_color: Color::Hex(0x45475A),
            border_highlight: Color::Hex(0x89B4FA),
            text_primary: Color::Hex(0xCDD6F4),
            text_secondary: Color::Hex(0xA6ADC8),
            accent_color: Color::Hex(0x89B4FA),
            connector_color: Color::Hex(0x585B70),
        }
    }

    /// Light theme color scheme.
    pub fn DefaultLight() -> Self {
        Self {
            background_root: Color::Hex(0xEFF1F5),
            background_branch: Color::Hex(0xE6E9EF),
            background_leaf: Color::Hex(0xDCE0E8),
            border_color: Color::Hex(0xCCD0DA),
            border_highlight: Color::Hex(0x1E66F5),
            text_primary: Color::Hex(0x4C4F69),
            text_secondary: Color::Hex(0x6C6F85),
            accent_color: Color::Hex(0x1E66F5),
            connector_color: Color::Hex(0x9CA0B0),
        }
    }

    /// Returns a depth-tinted background color for containers or cards.
    pub fn color_for_depth(&self, depth: u32, is_leaf: bool) -> Color {
        if is_leaf {
            return self.background_leaf;
        }
        match depth {
            0 => self.background_root,
            1 => self.background_branch,
            2 => Color::Hex(0x2A2C40),
            3 => Color::Hex(0x32344A),
            _ => self.background_branch,
        }
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Configuration options for 2D Xplr tree layout.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Cask2dOptions {
    pub mode: Cask2dLayoutMode,
    pub origin: Point,
    pub max_depth: u32,
    pub max_nodes: u32,
    pub node_width: f32,
    pub node_height: f32,
    pub horizontal_gap: f32,
    pub vertical_gap: f32,
    pub padding: Padding,
    pub colors: Cask2dColorScheme,
    pub show_icons: bool,
    pub show_size: bool,
}

impl Default for Cask2dOptions {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            mode: Cask2dLayoutMode::NestedBoxes,
            origin: Point::Zero(),
            max_depth: 4,
            max_nodes: 5000,
            node_width: 180.0,
            node_height: 36.0,
            horizontal_gap: 32.0,
            vertical_gap: 12.0,
            padding: Padding::All(8.0),
            colors: Cask2dColorScheme::DefaultDark(),
            show_icons: true,
            show_size: true,
        }
    }
}

impl Cask2dOptions {
    pub fn New() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn WithMode(mut self, mode: Cask2dLayoutMode) -> Self {
        self.mode = mode;
        self
    }

    pub fn WithOrigin(mut self, x: f32, y: f32) -> Self {
        self.origin = Point::New(x, y);
        self
    }

    pub fn WithMaxDepth(mut self, max_depth: u32) -> Self {
        self.max_depth = max_depth;
        self
    }

    pub fn WithMaxNodes(mut self, max_nodes: u32) -> Self {
        self.max_nodes = max_nodes;
        self
    }

    pub fn WithNodeSize(mut self, width: f32, height: f32) -> Self {
        self.node_width = width;
        self.node_height = height;
        self
    }

    pub fn WithGaps(mut self, horizontal: f32, vertical: f32) -> Self {
        self.horizontal_gap = horizontal;
        self.vertical_gap = vertical;
        self
    }

    pub fn WithPadding(mut self, padding: Padding) -> Self {
        self.padding = padding;
        self
    }

    pub fn WithColors(mut self, colors: Cask2dColorScheme) -> Self {
        self.colors = colors;
        self
    }

    pub fn WithColorScheme(self, colors: Cask2dColorScheme) -> Self {
        self.WithColors(colors)
    }

    pub fn WithIcons(mut self, show: bool) -> Self {
        self.show_icons = show;
        self
    }

    pub fn WithSizeDisplay(mut self, show: bool) -> Self {
        self.show_size = show;
        self
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Internal representation of an Xplr node during tree extraction.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
struct XplrTreeNode {
    id: String,
    name: String,
    path: String,
    is_leaf: bool,
    extension: String,
    size: u64,
    depth: u32,
    parent: Option<usize>,
    children: Vec<usize>,
}

/// A node positioned in 2D space.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Cask2dNode {
    pub id: String,
    pub name: String,
    pub path: String,
    pub is_leaf: bool,
    pub extension: String,
    pub size: u64,
    pub depth: u32,
    pub bounds: Rect,
    pub parent_index: Option<usize>,
    pub children_indices: Vec<usize>,
}

impl Cask2dNode {
    pub fn Name(&self) -> &str {
        &self.name
    }

    pub fn Path(&self) -> &str {
        &self.path
    }

    pub fn IsLeaf(&self) -> bool {
        self.is_leaf
    }

    pub fn Depth(&self) -> u32 {
        self.depth
    }

    pub fn Bounds(&self) -> Rect {
        self.bounds
    }

    pub fn Size(&self) -> u64 {
        self.size
    }

    pub fn Extension(&self) -> &str {
        &self.extension
    }

    pub fn ParentIndex(&self) -> Option<usize> {
        self.parent_index
    }

    pub fn ChildrenIndices(&self) -> &[usize] {
        &self.children_indices
    }

    /// Returns true if this node's 2D bounding box contains the specified point.
    pub fn ContainsPoint(&self, px: f32, py: f32) -> bool {
        self.bounds.Contains(px, py)
    }

    /// Returns the center point of this node's bounding box.
    pub fn Center(&self) -> Point {
        self.bounds.Center()
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Complete solved 2D layout for an Xplr tree hierarchy.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Cask2dLayout {
    pub bounds: Rect,
    pub nodes: Vec<Cask2dNode>,
    pub commands: Vec<CaskRenderCommand>,
    pub cask_tree: Option<Cask>,
}

impl Cask2dLayout {
    /// Solves the 2D layout for an `Xplr` tree starting at `root`.
    pub fn FromXplr(root: &dyn Xplr, options: &Cask2dOptions) -> Result<Self, String> {
        LayoutXplr2d(root, options)
    }

    /// Opens an `Xplr` tree from a URI through the `XplrRegistry` and solves its 2D layout.
    pub fn FromUri(uri: &str, options: &Cask2dOptions) -> Result<Self, String> {
        LayoutXplrUri(uri, options)
    }

    /// Total bounding rectangle encompassing all visual elements and padding.
    pub fn Bounds(&self) -> Rect {
        self.bounds
    }

    /// Slice of all positioned 2D nodes.
    pub fn Nodes(&self) -> &[Cask2dNode] {
        &self.nodes
    }

    /// Total number of positioned nodes.
    pub fn NodeCount(&self) -> usize {
        self.nodes.len()
    }

    /// Ordered draw commands in painter's order for rendering on a 2D canvas.
    pub fn Commands(&self) -> &[CaskRenderCommand] {
        &self.commands
    }

    /// Reference to the underlying `Cask` widget tree if constructed in `NestedBoxes` mode.
    pub fn CaskTree(&self) -> Option<&Cask> {
        self.cask_tree.as_ref()
    }

    /// Finds the topmost node containing the specified point.
    pub fn FindNodeAt(&self, point: Point) -> Option<&Cask2dNode> {
        self.nodes
            .iter()
            .rev()
            .find(|node| node.bounds.Contains(point.x, point.y))
    }

    /// Finds a node by its exact path or URI.
    pub fn FindNodeByPath(&self, path: &str) -> Option<&Cask2dNode> {
        self.nodes.iter().find(|node| node.path == path)
    }

    /// Finds the first node with a matching name.
    pub fn FindNodeByName(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Cask2dNode> {
        self.nodes.iter().find(|node| node.name == name)
    }

    /// Maximum depth found in the laid out tree.
    pub fn MaxDepth(&self) -> u32 {
        self.nodes.iter().map(|n| n.depth).max().unwrap_or(0)
    }

    /// Count of leaf nodes.
    pub fn LeafCount(&self) -> usize {
        self.nodes.iter().filter(|n| n.is_leaf).count()
    }

    /// Count of branch (directory/container) nodes.
    pub fn BranchCount(&self) -> usize {
        self.nodes.iter().filter(|n| !n.is_leaf).count()
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Solves 2D layout for an `Xplr` tree using the given options.
pub fn LayoutXplr2d(root: &dyn Xplr, options: &Cask2dOptions) -> Result<Cask2dLayout, String> {
    let mut tree_nodes = Vec::new();
    CollectXplrNode(root, 0, options.max_depth, options.max_nodes, &mut tree_nodes)?;

    if tree_nodes.is_empty() {
        return Ok(Cask2dLayout {
            bounds: Rect::Zero(),
            nodes: Vec::new(),
            commands: Vec::new(),
            cask_tree: None,
        });
    }

    match options.mode {
        Cask2dLayoutMode::NestedBoxes => LayoutNestedBoxes(&tree_nodes, options),
        Cask2dLayoutMode::HorizontalTree => LayoutHorizontalTree(&tree_nodes, options),
        Cask2dLayoutMode::VerticalTree => LayoutVerticalTree(&tree_nodes, options),
        Cask2dLayoutMode::IndentedList => LayoutIndentedList(&tree_nodes, options),
    }
}

/// Opens an `Xplr` tree from a URI string (e.g. `file://`, `settings://`, `example://`) and solves its 2D layout.
pub fn LayoutXplrUri(uri: &str, options: &Cask2dOptions) -> Result<Cask2dLayout, String> {
    let registry = XplrRegistry::New();
    let (_, branch) = registry.OpenRoot(uri)?;
    LayoutXplr2d(branch.as_ref(), options)
}

/// Constructs a pure `Cask` 2D widget hierarchy from an `Xplr` tree.
pub fn XplrToCask(root: &dyn Xplr, options: &Cask2dOptions) -> Result<Cask, String> {
    let mut tree_nodes = Vec::new();
    CollectXplrNode(root, 0, options.max_depth, options.max_nodes, &mut tree_nodes)?;
    if tree_nodes.is_empty() {
        return Ok(Cask::NewWindow(root.Path().to_string()));
    }
    Ok(BuildCaskNestedBoxes(0, &tree_nodes, options))
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Tree Ingestion Helper
//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn CollectXplrNode(
    node: &dyn Xplr,
    depth: u32,
    max_depth: u32,
    max_nodes: u32,
    nodes: &mut Vec<XplrTreeNode>,
) -> Result<usize, String> {
    if nodes.len() >= max_nodes as usize {
        return Err(format!("Xplr tree exceeds maximum limit of {} nodes", max_nodes));
    }
    let node_idx = nodes.len();
    let ext = node.Leaf().map(|l| l.Extension().to_string()).unwrap_or_default();
    let size = node.Leaf().map(|l| l.Size()).unwrap_or(0);

    nodes.push(XplrTreeNode {
        id: node.Path().to_string(),
        name: node.Name().to_string(),
        path: node.Path().to_string(),
        is_leaf: node.IsLeaf(),
        extension: ext,
        size,
        depth,
        parent: None,
        children: Vec::new(),
    });

    if depth < max_depth
        && !node.IsLeaf()
        && let Some(branch) = node.Branch()
        && let Ok(children) = branch.Children()
    {
        let mut sorted_indices: Vec<u32> = (0..children.Len()).collect();
        sorted_indices.sort_by(|&a, &b| {
            let ca = &children[a];
            let cb = &children[b];
            let a_is_dir = !ca.IsLeaf();
            let b_is_dir = !cb.IsLeaf();
            if a_is_dir != b_is_dir {
                b_is_dir.cmp(&a_is_dir)
            } else {
                ca.Name().to_lowercase().cmp(&cb.Name().to_lowercase())
            }
        });

        for idx in sorted_indices {
            if nodes.len() >= max_nodes as usize {
                break;
            }
            let child_node = &children[idx];
            let child_idx = CollectXplrNode(
                child_node.as_ref(),
                depth + 1,
                max_depth,
                max_nodes,
                nodes,
            )?;
            nodes[child_idx].parent = Some(node_idx);
            nodes[node_idx].children.push(child_idx);
        }
    }
    Ok(node_idx)
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mode 1: NestedBoxes Layout (Clay / Cask Container Model)
//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn BuildCaskNestedBoxes(
    node_idx: usize,
    nodes: &[XplrTreeNode],
    options: &Cask2dOptions,
) -> Cask {
    let node = &nodes[node_idx];
    let depth = node.depth;
    let colors = &options.colors;

    if node.is_leaf {
        let label_text = if options.show_icons {
            let icon = icon_for_node(&node.name, true, &node.extension);
            if options.show_size && node.size > 0 {
                format!("{} {} ({})", icon, node.name, format_size(node.size))
            } else {
                format!("{} {}", icon, node.name)
            }
        } else {
            node.name.clone()
        };

        Cask::NewWindow(node.id.clone())
            .WithDirection(LayoutDirection::TopToBottom)
            .WithSize(Sizing::Fit, Sizing::Fit)
            .WithPadding(Padding::Axes(8.0, 4.0))
            .WithBackground(colors.color_for_depth(depth, true))
            .WithBorder(colors.border_color, 1.0)
            .WithCornerRadius(4.0)
            .WithChild(
                Cask::NewLabel(format!("{}_lbl", node.id), label_text)
                    .WithFontSize(12.0)
                    .WithTextColor(colors.text_primary),
            )
    } else {
        let header_text = if options.show_icons {
            let icon = icon_for_node(&node.name, false, "");
            let count_str = format!("({} items)", node.children.len());
            format!("{} {} {}", icon, node.name, count_str)
        } else {
            format!("{} ({} items)", node.name, node.children.len())
        };

        let mut window = Cask::NewWindow(node.id.clone())
            .WithDirection(LayoutDirection::TopToBottom)
            .WithSize(Sizing::Fit, Sizing::Fit)
            .WithPadding(options.padding)
            .WithChildGap(options.vertical_gap)
            .WithBackground(colors.color_for_depth(depth, false))
            .WithBorder(colors.border_color, 1.5)
            .WithCornerRadius(6.0);

        window.AddChild(
            Cask::NewLabel(format!("{}_title", node.id), header_text)
                .WithFontSize(13.0)
                .WithTextColor(colors.accent_color),
        );

        if !node.children.is_empty() {
            let mut container = Cask::NewWindow(format!("{}_cnt", node.id))
                .WithDirection(LayoutDirection::TopToBottom)
                .WithSize(Sizing::Fit, Sizing::Fit)
                .WithPadding(Padding::Axes(4.0, 4.0))
                .WithChildGap(options.vertical_gap);

            for &child_idx in &node.children {
                let child_cask = BuildCaskNestedBoxes(child_idx, nodes, options);
                container.AddChild(child_cask);
            }
            window.AddChild(container);
        }

        window
    }
}

fn LayoutNestedBoxes(
    tree_nodes: &[XplrTreeNode],
    options: &Cask2dOptions,
) -> Result<Cask2dLayout, String> {
    let root_cask = BuildCaskNestedBoxes(0, tree_nodes, options);
    let commands = LayoutAndRenderCask(&root_cask, options.origin.x, options.origin.y);
    let total_bounds = root_cask.Bounds();

    let mut id_to_bounds = std::collections::HashMap::new();
    root_cask.TraverseDepth(|anc, enter| {
        if enter {
            let node = *anc.last().unwrap();
            id_to_bounds.insert(node.Id().to_string(), node.Bounds());
        }
        true
    });

    let mut result_nodes = Vec::with_capacity(tree_nodes.len());
    for tn in tree_nodes {
        let b = id_to_bounds.get(&tn.id).copied().unwrap_or(Rect::Zero());
        result_nodes.push(Cask2dNode {
            id: tn.id.clone(),
            name: tn.name.clone(),
            path: tn.path.clone(),
            is_leaf: tn.is_leaf,
            extension: tn.extension.clone(),
            size: tn.size,
            depth: tn.depth,
            bounds: b,
            parent_index: tn.parent,
            children_indices: tn.children.clone(),
        });
    }

    Ok(Cask2dLayout {
        bounds: total_bounds,
        nodes: result_nodes,
        commands,
        cask_tree: Some(root_cask),
    })
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mode 2: HorizontalTree Layout (Node-Link Left-to-Right)
//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn LayoutHorizontalTree(
    tree_nodes: &[XplrTreeNode],
    options: &Cask2dOptions,
) -> Result<Cask2dLayout, String> {
    let n = tree_nodes.len();
    let mut subtree_heights = vec![0.0f32; n];

    // Pass 1: Bottom-up subtree height calculation
    for i in (0..n).rev() {
        let node = &tree_nodes[i];
        if node.children.is_empty() {
            subtree_heights[i] = options.node_height + options.vertical_gap;
        } else {
            let mut sum_h = 0.0f32;
            for &c in &node.children {
                sum_h += subtree_heights[c];
            }
            subtree_heights[i] = sum_h.max(options.node_height + options.vertical_gap);
        }
    }

    // Pass 2: Top-down positioning
    let mut node_bounds = vec![Rect::Zero(); n];
    position_horizontal_node(0, options.origin.y, tree_nodes, &subtree_heights, options, &mut node_bounds);

    // Pass 3: Generate visual commands and connector lines
    let mut commands = Vec::new();
    let mut total_bounds = Rect::Zero();

    // 3a. Connector lines
    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        let pb = node_bounds[i];
        for &ci in &tn.children {
            let cb = node_bounds[ci];
            let start = Point::New(pb.Right(), pb.Center().y);
            let end = Point::New(cb.x, cb.Center().y);
            let mid_x = (start.x + end.x) * 0.5;

            // Orthogonal elbow connector
            commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Line {
                start,
                end: Point::New(mid_x, start.y),
                color: options.colors.connector_color,
                width: 1.5,
            });
            commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Line {
                start: Point::New(mid_x, start.y),
                end: Point::New(mid_x, end.y),
                color: options.colors.connector_color,
                width: 1.5,
            });
            commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Line {
                start: Point::New(mid_x, end.y),
                end,
                color: options.colors.connector_color,
                width: 1.5,
            });
        }
    }

    // 3b. Node cards
    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        let b = node_bounds[i];
        total_bounds = total_bounds.Union(&b);

        let bg_color = options.colors.color_for_depth(tn.depth, tn.is_leaf);
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Rectangle {
            bounds: b,
            color: bg_color,
            corner_radius: 5.0,
        });
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Border {
            bounds: b,
            color: options.colors.border_color,
            width: 1.0,
            corner_radius: 5.0,
        });

        let label = format_node_label(tn, options);
        let text_y = b.y + (b.height - 13.0) * 0.5;
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Text {
            bounds: Rect::New(b.x + 8.0, text_y, b.width - 16.0, 16.0),
            text: label,
            color: if tn.is_leaf {
                options.colors.text_primary
            } else {
                options.colors.accent_color
            },
            font_size: 12.0,
        });
    }

    let mut result_nodes = Vec::with_capacity(n);
    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        result_nodes.push(Cask2dNode {
            id: tn.id.clone(),
            name: tn.name.clone(),
            path: tn.path.clone(),
            is_leaf: tn.is_leaf,
            extension: tn.extension.clone(),
            size: tn.size,
            depth: tn.depth,
            bounds: node_bounds[i],
            parent_index: tn.parent,
            children_indices: tn.children.clone(),
        });
    }

    Ok(Cask2dLayout {
        bounds: total_bounds,
        nodes: result_nodes,
        commands,
        cask_tree: None,
    })
}

fn position_horizontal_node(
    idx: usize,
    y_start: f32,
    nodes: &[XplrTreeNode],
    subtree_h: &[f32],
    options: &Cask2dOptions,
    bounds: &mut [Rect],
) {
    let node = &nodes[idx];
    let x = options.origin.x + node.depth as f32 * (options.node_width + options.horizontal_gap);

    if node.children.is_empty() {
        let y = y_start + (subtree_h[idx] - options.node_height) * 0.5;
        bounds[idx] = Rect::New(x, y, options.node_width, options.node_height);
    } else {
        let mut cur_y = y_start;
        for &child in &node.children {
            position_horizontal_node(child, cur_y, nodes, subtree_h, options, bounds);
            cur_y += subtree_h[child];
        }
        let first_child_y = bounds[node.children[0]].Center().y;
        let last_child_y = bounds[*node.children.last().unwrap()].Center().y;
        let parent_center_y = (first_child_y + last_child_y) * 0.5;
        let y = parent_center_y - options.node_height * 0.5;
        bounds[idx] = Rect::New(x, y, options.node_width, options.node_height);
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mode 3: VerticalTree Layout (Node-Link Top-to-Bottom)
//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn LayoutVerticalTree(
    tree_nodes: &[XplrTreeNode],
    options: &Cask2dOptions,
) -> Result<Cask2dLayout, String> {
    let n = tree_nodes.len();
    let mut subtree_widths = vec![0.0f32; n];

    // Pass 1: Bottom-up subtree width calculation
    for i in (0..n).rev() {
        let node = &tree_nodes[i];
        if node.children.is_empty() {
            subtree_widths[i] = options.node_width + options.horizontal_gap;
        } else {
            let mut sum_w = 0.0f32;
            for &c in &node.children {
                sum_w += subtree_widths[c];
            }
            subtree_widths[i] = sum_w.max(options.node_width + options.horizontal_gap);
        }
    }

    // Pass 2: Top-down positioning
    let mut node_bounds = vec![Rect::Zero(); n];
    position_vertical_node(0, options.origin.x, tree_nodes, &subtree_widths, options, &mut node_bounds);

    // Pass 3: Visual commands & connectors
    let mut commands = Vec::new();
    let mut total_bounds = Rect::Zero();

    // 3a. Connector lines
    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        let pb = node_bounds[i];
        for &ci in &tn.children {
            let cb = node_bounds[ci];
            let start = Point::New(pb.Center().x, pb.Bottom());
            let end = Point::New(cb.Center().x, cb.y);
            let mid_y = (start.y + end.y) * 0.5;

            // Orthogonal elbow connector
            commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Line {
                start,
                end: Point::New(start.x, mid_y),
                color: options.colors.connector_color,
                width: 1.5,
            });
            commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Line {
                start: Point::New(start.x, mid_y),
                end: Point::New(end.x, mid_y),
                color: options.colors.connector_color,
                width: 1.5,
            });
            commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Line {
                start: Point::New(end.x, mid_y),
                end,
                color: options.colors.connector_color,
                width: 1.5,
            });
        }
    }

    // 3b. Node cards
    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        let b = node_bounds[i];
        total_bounds = total_bounds.Union(&b);

        let bg_color = options.colors.color_for_depth(tn.depth, tn.is_leaf);
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Rectangle {
            bounds: b,
            color: bg_color,
            corner_radius: 5.0,
        });
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Border {
            bounds: b,
            color: options.colors.border_color,
            width: 1.0,
            corner_radius: 5.0,
        });

        let label = format_node_label(tn, options);
        let text_y = b.y + (b.height - 13.0) * 0.5;
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Text {
            bounds: Rect::New(b.x + 8.0, text_y, b.width - 16.0, 16.0),
            text: label,
            color: if tn.is_leaf {
                options.colors.text_primary
            } else {
                options.colors.accent_color
            },
            font_size: 12.0,
        });
    }

    let mut result_nodes = Vec::with_capacity(n);
    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        result_nodes.push(Cask2dNode {
            id: tn.id.clone(),
            name: tn.name.clone(),
            path: tn.path.clone(),
            is_leaf: tn.is_leaf,
            extension: tn.extension.clone(),
            size: tn.size,
            depth: tn.depth,
            bounds: node_bounds[i],
            parent_index: tn.parent,
            children_indices: tn.children.clone(),
        });
    }

    Ok(Cask2dLayout {
        bounds: total_bounds,
        nodes: result_nodes,
        commands,
        cask_tree: None,
    })
}

fn position_vertical_node(
    idx: usize,
    x_start: f32,
    nodes: &[XplrTreeNode],
    subtree_w: &[f32],
    options: &Cask2dOptions,
    bounds: &mut [Rect],
) {
    let node = &nodes[idx];
    let y = options.origin.y + node.depth as f32 * (options.node_height + options.vertical_gap);

    if node.children.is_empty() {
        let x = x_start + (subtree_w[idx] - options.node_width) * 0.5;
        bounds[idx] = Rect::New(x, y, options.node_width, options.node_height);
    } else {
        let mut cur_x = x_start;
        for &child in &node.children {
            position_vertical_node(child, cur_x, nodes, subtree_w, options, bounds);
            cur_x += subtree_w[child];
        }
        let first_child_x = bounds[node.children[0]].Center().x;
        let last_child_x = bounds[*node.children.last().unwrap()].Center().x;
        let parent_center_x = (first_child_x + last_child_x) * 0.5;
        let x = parent_center_x - options.node_width * 0.5;
        bounds[idx] = Rect::New(x, y, options.node_width, options.node_height);
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Mode 4: IndentedList Layout (Hierarchical Outline with Guide Lines)
//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn LayoutIndentedList(
    tree_nodes: &[XplrTreeNode],
    options: &Cask2dOptions,
) -> Result<Cask2dLayout, String> {
    let n = tree_nodes.len();
    let mut commands = Vec::new();
    let mut node_bounds = vec![Rect::Zero(); n];
    let mut total_bounds = Rect::Zero();

    let row_height = options.node_height;
    let row_gap = options.vertical_gap;
    let indent_step = options.horizontal_gap;

    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        let x = options.origin.x + tn.depth as f32 * indent_step;
        let y = options.origin.y + i as f32 * (row_height + row_gap);
        let b = Rect::New(x, y, options.node_width, row_height);
        node_bounds[i] = b;
        total_bounds = total_bounds.Union(&b);

        let bg_color = options.colors.color_for_depth(tn.depth, tn.is_leaf);
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Rectangle {
            bounds: b,
            color: bg_color,
            corner_radius: 4.0,
        });
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Border {
            bounds: b,
            color: options.colors.border_color,
            width: 1.0,
            corner_radius: 4.0,
        });

        let label = format_node_label(tn, options);
        let text_y = b.y + (b.height - 13.0) * 0.5;
        commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Text {
            bounds: Rect::New(b.x + 8.0, text_y, b.width - 16.0, 16.0),
            text: label,
            color: if tn.is_leaf {
                options.colors.text_primary
            } else {
                options.colors.accent_color
            },
            font_size: 12.0,
        });
    }

    // Branch guide lines connecting parents to their children
    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        if !tn.children.is_empty() {
            let pb = node_bounds[i];
            let last_child_idx = *tn.children.last().unwrap();
            let last_cb = node_bounds[last_child_idx];
            let guide_x = pb.x + 12.0;

            // Vertical guide line
            commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Line {
                start: Point::New(guide_x, pb.Bottom()),
                end: Point::New(guide_x, last_cb.Center().y),
                color: options.colors.connector_color,
                width: 1.0,
            });

            // Horizontal stubs to each child
            for &ci in &tn.children {
                let cb = node_bounds[ci];
                commands.push(CaskRenderCommand::Line {
                    start: Point::New(guide_x, cb.Center().y),
                    end: Point::New(cb.x, cb.Center().y),
                    color: options.colors.connector_color,
                    width: 1.0,
                });
            }
        }
    }

    let mut result_nodes = Vec::with_capacity(n);
    for (i, tn) in tree_nodes.iter().enumerate() {
        result_nodes.push(Cask2dNode {
            id: tn.id.clone(),
            name: tn.name.clone(),
            path: tn.path.clone(),
            is_leaf: tn.is_leaf,
            extension: tn.extension.clone(),
            size: tn.size,
            depth: tn.depth,
            bounds: node_bounds[i],
            parent_index: tn.parent,
            children_indices: tn.children.clone(),
        });
    }

    Ok(Cask2dLayout {
        bounds: total_bounds,
        nodes: result_nodes,
        commands,
        cask_tree: None,
    })
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers: Labels, Icons, and Size Formatting
//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn format_node_label(node: &XplrTreeNode, options: &Cask2dOptions) -> String {
    if options.show_icons {
        let icon = icon_for_node(&node.name, node.is_leaf, &node.extension);
        if node.is_leaf && options.show_size && node.size > 0 {
            format!("{} {} ({})", icon, node.name, format_size(node.size))
        } else if !node.is_leaf && !node.children.is_empty() {
            format!("{} {} ({})", icon, node.name, node.children.len())
        } else {
            format!("{} {}", icon, node.name)
        }
    } else if node.is_leaf && options.show_size && node.size > 0 {
        format!("{} ({})", node.name, format_size(node.size))
    } else {
        node.name.clone()
    }
}

pub fn icon_for_node(name: &str, is_leaf: bool, ext: &str) -> &'static str {
    if !is_leaf {
        if name.contains(':') || name == "file://" {
            return "ðŸ’¾";
        }
        return "ðŸ“";
    }
    match ext.to_lowercase().as_str() {
        "rs" => "ðŸ¦€",
        "toml" | "json" | "yaml" | "yml" | "cfg" => "âš™",
        "md" | "txt" | "log" => "ðŸ“",
        "vcd" => "ðŸ“ˆ",
        "obj" | "pts" => "ðŸ§Š",
        "theme" => "ðŸŽ¨",
        "py" => "ðŸ",
        _ => "ðŸ“„",
    }
}

pub fn format_size(bytes: u64) -> String {
    if bytes < 1024 {
        format!("{} B", bytes)
    } else if bytes < 1024 * 1024 {
        format!("{:.1} KB", bytes as f64 / 1024.0)
    } else if bytes < 1024 * 1024 * 1024 {
        format!("{:.1} MB", bytes as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0))
    } else {
        format!("{:.2} GB", bytes as f64 / (1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0))
    }
}

// cask_scene.rs -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
//! Flat, breadth-first hierarchy and parent-contained 3D layout, independent of graphics APIs.

use crate::fenst::cask::{Cask, CaskKind};
use crate::silo::{Arr, Buff, IArr, Stash, USeg};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};

const MAX_HIERARCHY_NODES: u32 = 100_000;

pub(crate) fn    CheckCancelled( cancelled: &AtomicBool) -> Result<(), String>
{
    if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
    {
        return Err( "Loading cancelled.".into());
    }
    Ok( ())
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// A borrowed hierarchy source. Geometry owns labels and volumes, not the source tree.
/// Children must form a finite tree and are visited in presentation order.
pub trait ICaskHierarchy
{
    type Node: Copy;

    fn  Label( &self, node: Self::Node) -> String;

    /// Stops visiting children as soon as the visitor returns false.
    fn  SpanChildren( &self, node: Self::Node, visit: impl FnMut( Self::Node) -> bool);
}

impl<'a> ICaskHierarchy for &'a Cask
{
    type Node = &'a Cask;

    fn  Label( &self, node: Self::Node) -> String
    {
        if let  CaskKind::Label( text) = node.Kind()
        {
            return text.clone();
        }
        let mut label   = String::new();
        let mut hasLabel    = false;
        let children: Arr<'_, Cask>     = node.Children().into();
        children.Traverse( |child| {
            if let  CaskKind::Label( text) = child.Kind()
            {
                if hasLabel
                {
                    label.push( '\n');
                }
                label.push_str( text);
                hasLabel = true;
            }
        });
        return if hasLabel { label } else { node.Id().into() };
    }

    fn  SpanChildren( &self, node: Self::Node, mut visit: impl FnMut( Self::Node) -> bool)
    {
        let children: Arr<'a, Cask>     = node.Children().into();
        children.USeg().Span( |index| {
            let child   = &node.Children()[index as usize];
            if matches!( child.Kind(), CaskKind::Window)
            {
                return visit( child);
            }
            true
        });
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// A 3D viewing box specifying spatial bounds, depth range, and view-to-world scale.
#[derive( Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub struct ViewBox
{
    _ViewSize:                  [f32; 2],
    _WorldOffset:               [f32; 3],
    _ScaleViewToWorld:          f32,
    _Depth:                     f32,
    _MinPixelThreshold:         f32,
    _GpuMemoryThresholdBytes:   u64,
}

impl Default for ViewBox
{
    fn default() -> Self
    {
        return Self::New( [800.0, 600.0], [0.0; 3], 1.0);
    }
}

impl ViewBox
{
    pub const DEFAULT_MIN_PIXELS: f32 = 3.0;
    pub const DEPTH_ORDER_FACTOR: f32 = 100.0;
    pub const DEFAULT_GPU_MEMORY_THRESHOLD_BYTES: u64 = 32 * 1024 * 1024; // 32 MiB
    pub const GPU_BYTES_PER_NODE: u64 = 1832; // 8v*44B + 12t*12B + 12e*8B + 26p*44B + 4lv*24B

    /// Constructs a viewbox where depth is a couple orders of magnitude more than the max view dimension.
    /// `scale_view_to_world` is the scale from view coordinates (pixels) to world coordinates.
    pub fn  New( view_size: [f32; 2], world_offset: [f32; 3], scale_view_to_world: f32) -> Self
    {
        let width = if view_size[0].is_finite() && view_size[0] > 0.0 { view_size[0] } else { 1.0 };
        let height = if view_size[1].is_finite() && view_size[1] > 0.0 { view_size[1] } else { 1.0 };
        let maxDim = width.max( height);
        let depth = Self::DEPTH_ORDER_FACTOR * maxDim;
        let v2w = if scale_view_to_world.is_finite() && scale_view_to_world > 0.0 {
            scale_view_to_world
        } else {
            1.0
        };
        return Self {
            _ViewSize:                  [width, height],
            _WorldOffset:               world_offset,
            _ScaleViewToWorld:          v2w,
            _Depth:                     depth,
            _MinPixelThreshold:         Self::DEFAULT_MIN_PIXELS,
            _GpuMemoryThresholdBytes:   Self::DEFAULT_GPU_MEMORY_THRESHOLD_BYTES,
        };
    }

    /// Constructs a viewbox fitting given world bounds with the specified viewport dimensions.
    pub fn  FromWorld( view_size: [f32; 2], world_bounds: ( [f32; 3], [f32; 3])) -> Self
    {
        let center = [
            ( world_bounds.0[0] + world_bounds.1[0]) * 0.5,
            ( world_bounds.0[1] + world_bounds.1[1]) * 0.5,
            ( world_bounds.0[2] + world_bounds.1[2]) * 0.5,
        ];
        let extentX = ( world_bounds.1[0] - world_bounds.0[0]).max( 1.0);
        let extentY = ( world_bounds.1[1] - world_bounds.0[1]).max( 1.0);
        let vWidth = if view_size[0].is_finite() && view_size[0] > 0.0 { view_size[0] } else { 1.0 };
        let vHeight = if view_size[1].is_finite() && view_size[1] > 0.0 { view_size[1] } else { 1.0 };
        let scale_v2w = ( extentX / vWidth).max( extentY / vHeight);
        return Self::New( [vWidth, vHeight], center, scale_v2w);
    }

    pub fn  WithMinPixelThreshold( mut self, threshold: f32) -> Self
    {
        if threshold.is_finite() && threshold >= 0.0
        {
            self._MinPixelThreshold = threshold;
        }
        return self;
    }

    pub fn  WithDepth( mut self, depth: f32) -> Self
    {
        if depth.is_finite() && depth > 0.0
        {
            self._Depth = depth;
        }
        return self;
    }

    pub fn  WithDepthScale( mut self, factor: f32) -> Self
    {
        if factor.is_finite() && factor > 0.0
        {
            let maxDim = self._ViewSize[0].max( self._ViewSize[1]);
            self._Depth = factor * maxDim;
        }
        return self;
    }

    pub fn  WithGpuMemoryThreshold( mut self, bytes: u64) -> Self
    {
        if bytes > 0
        {
            self._GpuMemoryThresholdBytes = bytes;
        }
        return self;
    }

    pub fn  ViewSize( &self) -> [f32; 2] { return self._ViewSize; }
    pub fn  WorldOffset( &self) -> [f32; 3] { return self._WorldOffset; }
    pub fn  ScaleViewToWorld( &self) -> f32 { return self._ScaleViewToWorld; }
    pub fn  ScaleWorldToView( &self) -> f32 { return 1.0 / self._ScaleViewToWorld; }
    pub fn  Depth( &self) -> f32 { return self._Depth; }
    pub fn  MaxViewDimension( &self) -> f32 { return self._ViewSize[0].max( self._ViewSize[1]); }
    pub fn  MinPixelThreshold( &self) -> f32 { return self._MinPixelThreshold; }
    pub fn  GpuMemoryThreshold( &self) -> u64 { return self._GpuMemoryThresholdBytes; }

    /// Returns the maximum number of nodes permitted under the active GPU memory threshold.
    pub fn  MaxNodesForGpuBudget( &self) -> u32
    {
        let max_nodes = self._GpuMemoryThresholdBytes / Self::GPU_BYTES_PER_NODE;
        return max_nodes.min( MAX_HIERARCHY_NODES as u64 ) as u32;
    }

    /// Returns the viewbox bounds in view coordinates: ([0, 0, -depth/2], [width, height, depth/2]).
    pub fn  ViewBounds( &self) -> ( [f32; 3], [f32; 3])
    {
        return (
            [0.0, 0.0, -self._Depth * 0.5],
            [self._ViewSize[0], self._ViewSize[1], self._Depth * 0.5],
        );
    }

    /// Transforms an object's world box into view coordinates by applying the scale.
    pub fn  ScaledWorldBox( &self, world_origin: [f32; 3], world_size: [f32; 3]) -> ( [f32; 3], [f32; 3])
    {
        let scale = self.ScaleWorldToView();
        let scaled_size = [world_size[0] * scale, world_size[1] * scale, world_size[2] * scale];
        let scaled_origin = [
            ( world_origin[0] - self._WorldOffset[0]) * scale + self._ViewSize[0] * 0.5,
            ( world_origin[1] - self._WorldOffset[1]) * scale + self._ViewSize[1] * 0.5,
            ( world_origin[2] - self._WorldOffset[2]) * scale,
        ];
        return ( scaled_origin, scaled_size);
    }

    /// Computes the projected size of an entity in view pixels by applying scale to its world size.
    pub fn  ResolutionPixels( &self, world_size: [f32; 3]) -> f32
    {
        if !world_size.iter().all( |s| s.is_finite() && *s >= 0.0)
        {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let extent = world_size[0].max( world_size[1]).max( world_size[2]);
        return extent * self.ScaleWorldToView();
    }

    /// Tests whether the object's scaled world box overlaps this viewbox volume.
    pub fn  ContainsOrIntersects( &self, world_origin: [f32; 3], world_size: [f32; 3]) -> bool
    {
        let ( scaled_origin, scaled_size) = self.ScaledWorldBox( world_origin, world_size);
        let view_min = [0.0_f32, 0.0_f32, -self._Depth * 0.5];
        let view_max = [self._ViewSize[0], self._ViewSize[1], self._Depth * 0.5];

        let mut intersects = true;
        USeg::FromLen( 3).Span( |axis| {
            let i = axis as usize;
            let obj_min = scaled_origin[i];
            let obj_max = scaled_origin[i] + scaled_size[i];
            if !obj_min.is_finite() || !obj_max.is_finite()
               || obj_max < view_min[i] || obj_min > view_max[i]
            {
                intersects = false;
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        });
        return intersects;
    }

    /// Determines whether a geometric entity deserves further unfurling:
    /// Returns false if it lies outside the view box or has resolution less than the pixel threshold.
    pub fn  ShouldUnfurl( &self, world_origin: [f32; 3], world_size: [f32; 3]) -> bool
    {
        if !self.ContainsOrIntersects( world_origin, world_size)
        {
            return false;
        }
        if self.ResolutionPixels( world_size) < self._MinPixelThreshold
        {
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#[derive( Debug)]
pub struct CaskVolume
{
    _Name:     String,
    _Parent:   u32,
    _Children: USeg,
    _Depth:    u32,
    _Height:   u32,
    _Origin:   [f32; 3],
    _Size:     [f32; 3],
}

impl CaskVolume
{
    fn New( name: String, parent: u32, depth: u32) -> Self
    {
        Self { _Name:     name,
               _Parent:   parent,
               _Children: USeg::Empty(),
               _Depth:    depth,
               _Height:   0,
               _Origin:   [0.0; 3],
               _Size:     [0.0; 3], }
    }
    pub fn Name( &self) -> &str { &self._Name }
    pub fn Parent( &self) -> u32 { self._Parent }
    pub fn Depth( &self) -> u32 { self._Depth }
    pub fn Height( &self) -> u32 { self._Height }
    pub fn Origin( &self) -> [f32; 3] { self._Origin }
    pub fn Size( &self) -> [f32; 3] { self._Size }
    pub fn IsLeaf( &self) -> bool { self._Children.IsEmpty() }
    pub fn Children( &self) -> USeg { self._Children }
    pub fn DeservesUnfurling( &self, viewbox: &ViewBox) -> bool
    {
        return viewbox.ShouldUnfurl( self._Origin, self._Size);
    }
}

#[derive( Debug)]
pub struct CaskScene
{
    _Nodes: Buff<CaskVolume>,
}

impl CaskScene
{
    /// Enumerates every accessible entry. Symlinks are leaves. An incomplete scan is an error.
    pub fn Read( path: &Path, cancelled: &AtomicBool) -> Result<Self, String>
    {
        CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
        let mut paths = Stash::New();
        let mut nodes = Stash::New();
        paths.Push( path.to_path_buf());
        nodes.Push( CaskVolume::New( path.file_name()
                                       .unwrap_or( path.as_os_str())
                                       .to_string_lossy()
                                       .into_owned(),
                                   u32::MAX,
                                   1));
        let mut index = 0;
        while index < nodes.Size() {
            if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire) {
                return Err( "Loading cancelled.".into());
            }
            let current = &paths[index];
            let metadata = std::fs::symlink_metadata( current).map_err( |error| {
                               format!( "Cannot inspect {}: {error}", current.display())
                           })?;
            if metadata.is_dir() && !metadata.file_type().is_symlink() {
                let mut entries = Stash::New();
                let mut reader = std::fs::read_dir( current).map_err( |error| {
                                                               format!( "Cannot read {}: {error}",
                                                                       current.display())
                                                           })?;
                reader.try_for_each( |entry| -> Result<(), String> {
                    CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
                    if u64::from( nodes.Size()) + u64::from( entries.Size())
                       >= u64::from( MAX_HIERARCHY_NODES)
                    {
                        return Err( "Hierarchy exceeds 100,000 entries. Open a smaller root; no partial tree was loaded.".into());
                    }
                    entries.Push( entry.map_err( |error| error.to_string())?.path());
                    Ok( ())
                })?;
                entries.MutArr().QSort( |a, b| a < b);
                CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
                let first = nodes.Size();
                let depth = nodes[index]._Depth + 1;
                entries.Arr().USeg().Span( |entryIndex| {
                                 if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                                 {
                                     return false;
                                 }
                                 let entry = &entries[entryIndex];
                                 nodes.Push( CaskVolume::New( entry.file_name()
                                                                 .unwrap_or_default()
                                                                 .to_string_lossy()
                                                                 .into_owned(),
                                                            index,
                                                            depth));
                                 paths.Push( entry.clone());
                                 true
                             });
                CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
                nodes[index]._Children = USeg::WithLen( first, entries.Size());
            }
            index += 1;
        }
        let mut buff = nodes.ExtractBuff();
        Self::CalculateHeights( &mut buff, cancelled)?;
        Ok( Self { _Nodes: buff, })
    }

    /// Adapts existing in-memory casks without deriving geometry from their 2D bounds.
    pub fn FromRoot( root: &Cask) -> Result<Self, String>
    {
        return Self::FromHierarchy( &root, root, &AtomicBool::new( false));
    }

    /// Builds contiguous sibling ranges directly from a borrowed domain tree, without nested Casks.
    pub fn  FromHierarchy<H: ICaskHierarchy>( source: &H, root: H::Node, cancelled: &AtomicBool)
                                          -> Result<Self, String>
    {
        CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
        let mut sources = Stash::New();
        let mut nodes = Stash::New();
        sources.Push( root);
        nodes.Push( CaskVolume::New( source.Label( root), u32::MAX, 1));
        let mut index = 0;
        while index < sources.Size() {
            CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
            let first = nodes.Size();
            let depth = nodes[index]._Depth + 1;
            let mut exceeded = false;
            source.SpanChildren( sources[index], |child| {
                if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                {
                    return false;
                }
                if nodes.Size() >= MAX_HIERARCHY_NODES
                {
                    exceeded = true;
                    return false;
                }
                nodes.Push( CaskVolume::New( source.Label( child), index, depth));
                sources.Push( child);
                !cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
            });
            CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
            if exceeded
            {
                return Err( "Hierarchy exceeds 100,000 entries. Open a smaller root; no partial tree was loaded.".into());
            }
            nodes[index]._Children = USeg::WithLen( first, nodes.Size() - first);
            index += 1;
        }
        let mut buff = nodes.ExtractBuff();
        Self::CalculateHeights( &mut buff, cancelled)?;
        Ok( Self { _Nodes: buff, })
    }

    fn CalculateHeights( nodes: &mut Buff<CaskVolume>, cancelled: &AtomicBool) -> Result<(), String>
    {
        nodes.Arr().USeg().Span( |step| {
            if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
            {
                return false;
            }
            let index = nodes.Size() - step - 1;
            let children = nodes[index]._Children;
            if !children.IsEmpty() {
                let mut maxChild = 0;
                children.Span( |child| {
                    if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                    {
                        return false;
                    }
                    maxChild = maxChild.max( nodes[child]._Height);
                    true
                });
                nodes[index]._Height = maxChild + 1;
            }
            true
        });
        CheckCancelled( cancelled)
    }

    pub fn Nodes( &self) -> Arr<'_, CaskVolume> { self._Nodes.Arr() }
    pub fn MaxDepth( &self) -> u32 { self._Nodes[self._Nodes.Size() - 1]._Depth }
    pub fn Bounds( &self) -> ( [f32; 3], [f32; 3]) { ( [0.0; 3], self._Nodes[0]._Size) }

    /// Checks whether the entity at `index` deserves further child unfurling.
    pub fn  DeservesUnfurling( &self, index: u32, viewbox: &ViewBox) -> bool
    {
        if index >= self._Nodes.Size()
        {
            return false;
        }
        let node = &self._Nodes[index];
        return node.DeservesUnfurling( viewbox);
    }

    /// Prunes children of entities that do not deserve further unfurling (outside viewbox or sub-pixel).
    pub fn  Unfurl( &self, viewbox: &ViewBox, cancelled: &AtomicBool) -> Result<Self, String>
    {
        CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
        if self._Nodes.IsEmpty()
        {
            return Ok( Self { _Nodes: Buff::New() });
        }
        if self._Nodes[0]._Size.iter().any( |s| *s <= 0.0)
        {
            return Err( "Cannot unfurl a CaskScene before layout.".into());
        }

        let mut sources = Stash::New();
        let mut nodes = Stash::New();

        sources.Push( 0_u32);
        let root = &self._Nodes[0];
        nodes.Push( CaskVolume {
            _Name:     root._Name.clone(),
            _Parent:   u32::MAX,
            _Children: USeg::Empty(),
            _Depth:    1,
            _Height:   0,
            _Origin:   root._Origin,
            _Size:     root._Size,
        });

        let gpu_node_limit = viewbox.MaxNodesForGpuBudget();
        let mut index = 0_u32;
        let mut cur_depth = 1_u32;
        let mut cur_depth_unfurled = false;
        let mut depth_cutoff_reached = false;

        while index < sources.Size() {
            CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
            let origIdx = sources[index];
            let origNode = &self._Nodes[origIdx];
            let node_depth = nodes[index]._Depth;

            if node_depth != cur_depth
            {
                // If the previous depth level had nodes, but NONE of them deserved unfurling,
                // that depth is uniformly heuristically assumed to hold for all deeper levels.
                if !cur_depth_unfurled
                {
                    depth_cutoff_reached = true;
                }
                cur_depth = node_depth;
                cur_depth_unfurled = false;
            }

            if !depth_cutoff_reached
               && origNode.DeservesUnfurling( viewbox)
               && !origNode._Children.IsEmpty()
            {
                // Account for GPU memory threshold: if adding these children would exceed
                // the GPU budget, stop unfurling this branch so performance remains manageable.
                if nodes.Size() + origNode._Children.Len() <= gpu_node_limit
                {
                    cur_depth_unfurled = true;
                    let first = nodes.Size();
                    let depth = node_depth + 1;
                    let mut exceeded = false;

                    origNode._Children.Span( |childOrigIdx| {
                        if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                        {
                            return false;
                        }
                        if nodes.Size() >= MAX_HIERARCHY_NODES
                        {
                            exceeded = true;
                            return false;
                        }
                        let childNode = &self._Nodes[childOrigIdx];
                        nodes.Push( CaskVolume {
                            _Name:     childNode._Name.clone(),
                            _Parent:   index,
                            _Children: USeg::Empty(),
                            _Depth:    depth,
                            _Height:   0,
                            _Origin:   childNode._Origin,
                            _Size:     childNode._Size,
                        });
                        sources.Push( childOrigIdx);
                        return true;
                    });

                    CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
                    if exceeded
                    {
                        return Err( "Hierarchy exceeds 100,000 entries. Open a smaller root; no partial tree was loaded.".into());
                    }
                    nodes[index]._Children = USeg::WithLen( first, nodes.Size() - first);
                }
            }
            index += 1;
        }

        let mut buff = nodes.ExtractBuff();
        Self::CalculateHeights( &mut buff, cancelled)?;
        return Ok( Self { _Nodes: buff });
    }

    /// Text baselines follow X. Leaves have equal Y/Z extents. Parents pack in Y and Z.
    pub fn Layout( &mut self, cancelled: &AtomicBool, mut measure: impl FnMut( &str) -> [f32; 2])
                 -> Result<(), String>
    {
        const PAD: f32 = 4.0;
        const GAP: f32 = 4.0;
        self._Nodes.Arr().USeg().Span( |index| {
                                if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                                {
                                    return false;
                                }
                                let node = &mut self._Nodes[index];
                                let text = measure( &node._Name);
                                let height = text[1].max( 1.0) + 2.0 * PAD;
                                node._Size = [text[0].max( 1.0) + 2.0 * PAD, height, height];
                                node._Origin = [0.0; 3];
                                true
                            });
        CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
        self._Nodes.Arr().USeg().Span( |step| {
                                    if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                                    {
                                        return false;
                                    }
                                    let index = self._Nodes.Size() - step - 1;
                                    let children = self._Nodes[index]._Children;
                                    if children.IsEmpty() {
                                        return true;
                                    }
                                    let title = self._Nodes[index]._Size;
                                    let mut width = title[0];
                                    let mut total = 0.0;
                                    let mut maxHeight = 0.0_f32;
                                    children.Span( |child| {
                                                if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                                                {
                                                    return false;
                                                }
                                                let size = self._Nodes[child]._Size;
                                                width = width.max( size[0] + 2.0 * PAD);
                                                total += size[1] + GAP;
                                                maxHeight = maxHeight.max( size[1]);
                                                true
                                            });
                                    // Bounded candidate search: balanced footprints without quadratic packing work.
                                    let mut best = ( f32::INFINITY, maxHeight, [0.0; 3]);
                                    USeg::FromLen( 17).Span( |candidate| {
                                                         if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                                                         {
                                                             return false;
                                                         }
                                                         let limit = maxHeight
                                                                     + ( total - maxHeight)
                                                                       * candidate as f32
                                                                       / 16.0;
                                                         let mut y = 0.0_f32;
                                                         let mut z = PAD;
                                                         let mut layerDepth = 0.0_f32;
                                                         let mut height = 0.0_f32;
                                                         children.Span( |child| {
                                                                     if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                                                                     {
                                                                         return false;
                                                                     }
                                                                     let size =
                                                                         self._Nodes[child]._Size;
                                                                     if y > 0.0
                                                                        && y + size[1] > limit
                                                                     {
                                                                         z += layerDepth + GAP;
                                                                         layerDepth = 0.0;
                                                                         y = 0.0;
                                                                     }
                                                                     height =
                                                                         height.max( y + size[1]);
                                                                     y += size[1] + GAP;
                                                                     layerDepth =
                                                                         layerDepth.max( size[2]);
                                                                     true
                                                                 });
                                                         let size = [width,
                                                                     title[1] + height + PAD,
                                                                     z + layerDepth + PAD];
                                                         let longest =
                                                             size[0].max( size[1]).max( size[2]);
                                                         let shortest =
                                                             size[0].min( size[1]).min( size[2]);
                                                         let score =
                                                             longest / shortest
                                                             + size[0] * size[1] * size[2] * 1e-9;
                                                         if score < best.0 {
                                                             best = ( score, limit, size);
                                                         }
                                                         true
                                                     });
                                    let mut y = 0.0;
                                    let mut z = PAD;
                                    let mut layerDepth = 0.0_f32;
                                    children.Span( |child| {
                                                if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                                                {
                                                    return false;
                                                }
                                                let size = self._Nodes[child]._Size;
                                                if y > 0.0 && y + size[1] > best.1 {
                                                    z += layerDepth + GAP;
                                                    layerDepth = 0.0;
                                                    y = 0.0;
                                                }
                                                self._Nodes[child]._Origin = [PAD, title[1] + y, z];
                                                y += size[1] + GAP;
                                                layerDepth = layerDepth.max( size[2]);
                                                true
                                            });
                                    self._Nodes[index]._Size = best.2;
                                    true
                                });
        CheckCancelled( cancelled)?;
        self._Nodes.Arr().USeg().Span( |index| {
                                    if cancelled.load( Ordering::Acquire)
                                    {
                                        return false;
                                    }
                                    let parent = self._Nodes[index]._Parent;
                                    if parent != u32::MAX {
                                        let offset = self._Nodes[parent]._Origin;
                                        let origin = self._Nodes[index]._Origin;
                                        self._Nodes[index]._Origin =
                                            std::array::from_fn( |axis| origin[axis] + offset[axis]);
                                    }
                                    true
                                });
        CheckCancelled( cancelled)
    }
}

// example.rs -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

use crate::cove::{TestContext, TestKind};
use crate::fenst::{BranchXplr, Xplr, XplrProvider};
use crate::silo::{Buff, Stash};

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

pub type XplrExampleResult = Result<Box<dyn Xplr + Send>, String>;

/// An example registered with both Cove and the interactive explorer.
pub struct XplrExample
{
    pub suite: &'static str,
    pub name:  &'static str,
    pub func:  fn(&mut TestContext) -> XplrExampleResult,
}

inventory::collect!(XplrExample);

impl XplrExample
{
    pub fn Registered() -> Buff<&'static Self>
    {
        let mut examples = Stash::New();
        inventory::iter::<Self>.into_iter()
                               .for_each(|example| examples.Push(example));
        examples.MutArr()
                .QSort(|a, b| (a.suite, a.name) < (b.suite, b.name));
        return examples.IntoBuff();
    }

    /// Assertions stay enabled in the UI. Failed runs never publish their root.
    pub fn Run(&self) -> XplrExampleResult
    {
        let mut ctx = TestContext::new(self.suite, self.name, TestKind::Example, 1, true, false);
        let result = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
                                                  (self.func)(&mut ctx)
                                              })).map_err(|panic| {
                                                     let message =
                                                         panic.downcast_ref::<String>()
                                                              .map(String::as_str)
                                                              .or_else(|| {
                                                                  panic.downcast_ref::<&str>()
                                                                       .copied()
                                                              })
                                                              .unwrap_or("Unknown panic");
                                                     return format!("Example panicked: {message}");
                                                 })?;
        if ctx.fail_count != 0 {
            return Err(format!("{} assertion(s) failed", ctx.fail_count));
        }
        return result;
    }
}

impl Xplr for XplrExample
{
    fn Name(&self) -> &str { self.name }
    fn Path(&self) -> &str { self.name }
    fn IsLeaf(&self) -> bool { false }
    fn Branch(&self) -> Option<&dyn BranchXplr> { Some(self) }
}

impl BranchXplr for XplrExample
{
    fn Children(&self) -> Result<Buff<Box<dyn Xplr>>, String>
    {
        let res = self.Run()?;
        let mut stash = Stash::New();
        stash.Push(res as Box<dyn Xplr>);
        Ok(stash.IntoBuff())
    }

    fn ChildCount(&self) -> Result<u32, String>
    {
        Ok(1)
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// An Xplr node representing an individual test example under `example://<suite>/<name>`.
pub struct ExampleItemXplr
{
    _Suite:   String,
    _Name:    String,
    _Path:    String,
    _Example: &'static XplrExample,
}

impl ExampleItemXplr
{
    pub fn New(example: &'static XplrExample) -> Self
    {
        Self {
            _Suite:   example.suite.to_string(),
            _Name:    example.name.to_string(),
            _Path:    format!("example://{}/{}", example.suite, example.name),
            _Example: example,
        }
    }
}

impl Xplr for ExampleItemXplr
{
    fn Name(&self) -> &str { &self._Name }
    fn Path(&self) -> &str { &self._Path }
    fn IsLeaf(&self) -> bool { false }
    fn Branch(&self) -> Option<&dyn BranchXplr> { Some(self) }
}

impl BranchXplr for ExampleItemXplr
{
    fn Children(&self) -> Result<Buff<Box<dyn Xplr>>, String>
    {
        let res = self._Example.Run()?;
        let mut stash = Stash::New();
        stash.Push(res as Box<dyn Xplr>);
        Ok(stash.IntoBuff())
    }

    fn ChildCount(&self) -> Result<u32, String>
    {
        Ok(1)
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// An Xplr branch representing an entire test suite under `example://<suite>`.
pub struct ExampleSuiteXplr
{
    _Name:     String,
    _Path:     String,
    _Examples: Buff<&'static XplrExample>,
}

impl ExampleSuiteXplr
{
    pub fn New(suite: &str) -> Self
    {
        let all = XplrExample::Registered();
        let mut matching = Stash::New();
        all.Traverse(|ex| {
            if ex.suite == suite {
                matching.Push(*ex);
            }
        });
        Self {
            _Name:     suite.to_string(),
            _Path:     format!("example://{}", suite),
            _Examples: matching.IntoBuff(),
        }
    }
}

impl Xplr for ExampleSuiteXplr
{
    fn Name(&self) -> &str { &self._Name }
    fn Path(&self) -> &str { &self._Path }
    fn IsLeaf(&self) -> bool { false }
    fn Branch(&self) -> Option<&dyn BranchXplr> { Some(self) }
}

impl BranchXplr for ExampleSuiteXplr
{
    fn Children(&self) -> Result<Buff<Box<dyn Xplr>>, String>
    {
        let mut stash = Stash::New();
        self._Examples.Traverse(|ex| {
            stash.Push(Box::new(ExampleItemXplr::New(ex)) as Box<dyn Xplr>);
        });
        Ok(stash.IntoBuff())
    }

    fn ChildCount(&self) -> Result<u32, String>
    {
        Ok(self._Examples.Len())
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// The root Xplr branch representing all registered examples under `example://`.
pub struct ExamplesRootXplr
{
    _Path: String,
}

impl ExamplesRootXplr
{
    pub fn New() -> Self
    {
        Self { _Path: "example://".to_string() }
    }

    pub fn Suites() -> Buff<String>
    {
        let all = XplrExample::Registered();
        let mut suites = Stash::New();
        let mut last = "";
        all.Traverse(|ex| {
            if ex.suite != last {
                last = ex.suite;
                suites.Push(ex.suite.to_string());
            }
        });
        suites.IntoBuff()
    }
}

impl Default for ExamplesRootXplr
{
    fn default() -> Self { Self::New() }
}

impl Xplr for ExamplesRootXplr
{
    fn Name(&self) -> &str { "Examples" }
    fn Path(&self) -> &str { &self._Path }
    fn IsLeaf(&self) -> bool { false }
    fn Branch(&self) -> Option<&dyn BranchXplr> { Some(self) }
}

impl BranchXplr for ExamplesRootXplr
{
    fn Children(&self) -> Result<Buff<Box<dyn Xplr>>, String>
    {
        let suites = Self::Suites();
        let mut stash = Stash::New();
        suites.Traverse(|s| {
            stash.Push(Box::new(ExampleSuiteXplr::New(s)) as Box<dyn Xplr>);
        });
        Ok(stash.IntoBuff())
    }

    fn ChildCount(&self) -> Result<u32, String>
    {
        Ok(Self::Suites().Len())
    }
}

//-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
/// Example provider resolving `example://` URIs.
pub struct ExampleProvider;

impl ExampleProvider
{
    pub fn New() -> Self { Self }
}

impl Default for ExampleProvider
{
    fn default() -> Self { Self::New() }
}

impl XplrProvider for ExampleProvider
{
    fn Scheme(&self) -> &str { "example" }

    fn OpenRoot(&self, uri: &str) -> Result<Box<dyn BranchXplr>, String>
    {
        let stripped = uri.strip_prefix("example://").unwrap_or(uri);
        let trimmed = stripped.trim_matches('/');
        if trimmed.is_empty() {
            return Ok(Box::new(ExamplesRootXplr::New()));
        }
        if let Some((suite, name)) = trimmed.split_once('/') {
            let all = XplrExample::Registered();
            let mut found = None;
            all.Traverse(|ex| {
                if ex.suite == suite && ex.name == name {
                    found = Some(*ex);
                }
            });
            if let Some(ex) = found {
                return Ok(Box::new(ExampleItemXplr::New(ex)));
            }
            return Err(format!("Example not found: {}/{}", suite, name));
        }
        Ok(Box::new(ExampleSuiteXplr::New(trimmed)))
    }
}
